SELECT m.meeting_id, COUNT(sim.staff_id) AS num_staff FROM Meetings m JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON m.meeting_id = sim.meeting_id GROUP BY m.meeting_id;
SELECT s.staff_id, COUNT(*) AS num_attended FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON s.staff_id = sim.staff_id WHERE sim.meeting_id IN ( SELECT m.meeting_id FROM Meetings m GROUP BY m.meeting_id HAVING COUNT(sim.meeting_id) = ( SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Staff_in_Meetings sm WHERE sm.meeting_id = m.meeting_id AND sm.staff_id = s.staff_id ) ) GROUP BY s.staff_id;
SELECT s.staff_id FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sm ON s.staff_id = sm.staff_id WHERE sm.meeting_id IN ( SELECT m.meeting_id FROM Meetings m JOIN Clients c ON m.client_id = c.client_id WHERE c.agency_id = 1 GROUP BY m.meeting_id HAVING COUNT(m.meeting_id) = ( SELECT MIN(count(meeting_id)) FROM Meetings WHERE client_id IN ( SELECT client_id FROM Clients WHERE agency_id = 1 ) ) )
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Staff AS T1 INNER JOIN Staff_in_Meetings AS T2 ON T1.staff_id = T2.staff_id
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT sm.staff_id) AS num_distinct_staff FROM Staff_in_Meetings sm JOIN Staff s ON sm.staff_id = s.staff_id;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT staff_id) AS num_staff_not_attended FROM Staff WHERE staff_id NOT IN (SELECT staff_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings);
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT staff_id) AS no_meetings FROM Staff WHERE staff_id NOT IN (SELECT staff_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings)
SELECT T1.agency_id, T1.client_details FROM Agencies AS T1 JOIN Clients AS T2 ON T1.agency_id = T2.agency_id WHERE T2.client_id IN (SELECT client_id FROM Invoices) OR T2.client_id IN (SELECT client_id FROM Meetings) OR EXISTS (SELECT * FROM Staff_in_Meetings AS SM JOIN Meetings AS M ON SM.meeting_id = M.meeting_id WHERE M.client_id = T2.client_id AND SM.staff_id IS NOT NULL);
SELECT T1.client_id, T1.client_details FROM Clients AS T1 INNER JOIN Meetings AS T2 ON T1.client_id = T2.client_id WHERE T2.billable_yn = '1' OR EXISTS (SELECT * FROM Invoices AS T3 WHERE T3.client_id = T1.client_id AND T3.invoice_status IN ('Working', 'Starting'))
SELECT s.staff_id, s.staff_details FROM Staff s JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON s.staff_id = sim.staff_id WHERE EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM Staff_in_Meetings simm WHERE simm.staff_id = s.staff_id AND simm.meeting_id IN ( SELECT meeting_id FROM Meetings WHERE other_details LIKE '%s%' ) )
SELECT S.staff_id, S.staff_details FROM Staff AS S JOIN Staff_in_Meetings AS SIM ON S.staff_id = SIM.staff_id WHERE SIM.meeting_id IN ( SELECT meeting_id FROM Staff_in_Meetings WHERE staff_id IN ( SELECT staff_id FROM Staff WHERE staff_details LIKE '%s%' ) )
SELECT T1.agency_id, T1.sic_code, T2.client_id FROM Agencies AS T1 JOIN Clients AS T2 ON T1.agency_id = T2.agency_id WHERE T2.client_id IN ( SELECT M.client_id FROM Meetings AS M WHERE M.billable_yn = '1' ) AND EXISTS ( SELECT I.invoice_id FROM Invoices AS I WHERE I.client_id = T2.client_id );
SELECT C.client_id, C.sic_code, A.agency_id FROM Clients C JOIN Meetings M ON C.client_id = M.client_id JOIN Payments P ON M.invoice_id = P.invoice_id JOIN Staff S ON M.staff_id = S.staff_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings SM ON M.meeting_id = SM.meeting_id WHERE M.billable_yn = '1' AND P.payment_id IS NOT NULL;
SELECT M.start_date_time AS start_time, M.end_date_time AS end_time, C.client_details, S.staff_details FROM Meetings M JOIN Clients C ON M.client_id = C.client_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings SIM ON M.meeting_id = SIM.meeting_id JOIN Staff S ON SIM.staff_id = S.staff_id;
SELECT m.start_date_time AS start_time, m.end_date_time AS end_time, c.client_details AS client_name, s.staff_details AS staff_name FROM Meetings m JOIN Clients c ON m.client_id = c.client_id JOIN Staff_in_Meetings sim ON m.meeting_id = sim.meeting_id JOIN Staff s ON sim.staff_id = s.staff_id;
